## 區域統計(農藥分析工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　0e6893b2d4f11953ac-35

**english**　Zonal Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- Raster 分析工具箱
- 資料工具摘要

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html

**text**　#----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Name: ZonalStatistics_Ex_02.py
# Description: Calculates the maximum sea-surface temperature for different
#              ecological zones.
# Requirements: ArcGIS Image Server

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
inputZoneRasterOrFeatures = 'https://MyPortal.esri.com/server/rest/services/Hosted/zones/ImageServer'
inputValueRaster = 'https://MyPortal.esri.com/server/rest/services/Hosted/seaSurfaceTemperature/ImageServer'
outputName = 'outSSTRaster'
zoneField = 'temperature'
statisticType = 'MAXIMUM' 
ignoreNodata = ''
processAsMultidimensional = 'ALL_SLICES'

# Run Multidimensional ZonalStatistics operation
arcpy.ra.ZonalStatistics(inputZoneRasterOrFeatures, zoneField, 
                         inputValueRaster, outputName, statisticType, 
                         ignoreNodata, processAsMultidimensional)

## 區域統計(農藥分析工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Zonal Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- Raster 分析工具箱
- 資料工具摘要

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html

**id**　0e6893b2d4f11953ac-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "inputZoneRasterOrFeatures", "label_zh": "輸入區光或特性", "label_en": "Input Zone Raster or Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input that defines the zones. Both raster and feature data can be used for the zone input."}, {"parameter": "zoneField", "label_zh": "區域欄位", "label_en": "Zone Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The field that contains the values that define each zone. It can be an integer or a string field of the zone dataset."}, {"parameter": "inputValueRaster", "label_zh": "投入值", "label_en": "Input Value Raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "The raster that contains the values for which to summarize a statistic."}, {"parameter": "outputName", "label_zh": "輸出名稱", "label_en": "Output Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output raster service. If the image service layer already exists, you will be prompted to provide another name."}, {"parameter": "statisticType", "label_zh": "統計類型 (備選)", "label_en": "Statistic Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the statistic type that will be calculated. The available options when the value raster is integer are Mean, Majority, Majority count, Majority percentage, Maximum, Median, Minimum, Minority, Minority count, Minority percentage, Percentile, Range, Standard deviation, Sum, and Variety. If the value raster is float, the options are Mean, Maximum, Median, Minimum, Percentile, Range, Standard deviation, and Sum. Mean—The average of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. This is the default. Majority—The value that occurs most often for all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Majority count—The frequency of all cells that contain the majority value in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Majority percentage—The percentage of cells that contain the majority value in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Maximum—The largest value of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Median—The median value of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Minimum—The smallest value of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Minority—The value that occurs least often for all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Minority count—The frequency of all cells that contain the minority value in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Minority percentage—The percentage of cells that contain the minority value in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Percentile—The percentile of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. The 90th percentile is calculated by default. You can specify other values (from 0 to 100) using the Percentile Value parameter. Range—The difference between the largest and smallest value of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Standard deviation—The standard deviation of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Sum—The total value of all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated. Variety—The number of unique values for all cells in the value raster that belong to the same zone as the output cell will be calculated."}, {"parameter": "ignoreNodata", "label_zh": "計算時忽略無資料( 可選)", "label_en": "Ignore NoData in Calculations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether NoData values in the value input will be ignored in the results of the zones that they fall within. Checked—Within any particular zone, only cells that have a value in the input value raster will be used in determining the output value for that zone. NoData cells in the value raster will be ignored in the statistic calculation. This is the default. Unchecked—Within any particular zone, if NoData cells exist in the value raster, they will not be ignored and their existence indicates that there is insufficient information to perform statistical calculations for all the cells in that zone. Consequently, the entire zone will receive the NoData value."}, {"parameter": "processAsMultidimensional", "label_zh": "作為多層面(可選)進程", "label_en": "Process as Multidimensional (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies how the input rasters will be processed if they are multidimensional. Checked—Statistics will be calculated from the current slice of the input multidimensional dataset. This is the default. Unchecked—Statistics will be calculated for all dimensions (such as time or depth) from all slices of the multidimensional input dataset."}, {"parameter": "percentileValue", "label_zh": "百分比值(可選)", "label_en": "Percentile Value (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The percentile that will be calculated. The default is 90, indicating the 90th percentile. The values can range from 0 to 100. The 0th percentile is essentially equivalent to the minimum statistic, and the 100th percentile is equivalent to maximum. A value of 50 will produce essentially the same result as the median statistic. This parameter is only available when calculating percentile."}, {"parameter": "percentileInterpolationType", "label_zh": "百分比內插類型(可選)", "label_en": "Percentile Interpolation Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method of interpolation that will be used when the percentile value falls between two cell values from the input value raster. Auto-detect—If the input value raster is of integer pixel type, the Nearest method will be used. If the input value raster is of floating point pixel type, the Linear method will be used. This is the default. Nearest—The nearest available value to the desired percentile will be used. In this case, the output pixel type is the same as that of the input value raster. Linear—The weighted average of the two surrounding values from the desired percentile will be used. In this case, the output pixel type is floating point."}, {"parameter": "circularCalculation", "label_zh": "計算循環統計(可選)", "label_en": "Calculate Circular Statistics (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies how the statistics type will be calculated. Checked—Arithmetic statistics will be calculated. This is the default. Unchecked—Circular statistics that are appropriate for cyclic quantities will be calculated, such as compass direction in degrees, daytimes, and fractional parts of real numbers."}, {"parameter": "circularWrapValue", "label_zh": "圓環環環繞值( 可選)", "label_en": "Circular Wrap Value (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The highest possible value (upper bound) in the cyclic data. It is a positive number, with a default value of 360. This value also represents the same quantity as the lowest possible value (lower bound). This parameter is only applicable when circular statistics are calculated."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.ra.ZonalStatistics(inputZoneRasterOrFeatures, zoneField, inputValueRaster, outputName, {statisticType}, {ignoreNodata}, {processAsMultidimensional}, {percentileValue}, {percentileInterpolationType}, {circularCalculation}, {circularWrapValue})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：inputZoneRasterOrFeatures、zoneField、inputValueRaster、outputName、statisticType、ignoreNodata、processAsMultidimensional、percentileValue、percentileInterpolationType、circularCalculation、circularWrapValue。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 區域統計(農藥分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- Raster 分析工具箱
- 資料工具摘要

**english**　Zonal Statistics

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html

**id**　0e6893b2d4f11953ac-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Zonal Statistics，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入區光或特性 [inputZoneRasterOrFeatures]、區域欄位 [zoneField]、投入值 [inputValueRaster]、輸出名稱 [outputName]、統計類型 (備選) [statisticType]、計算時忽略無資料( 可選) [ignoreNodata]、作為多層面(可選)進程 [processAsMultidimensional]、百分比值(可選) [percentileValue]、百分比內插類型(可選) [percentileInterpolationType]、計算循環統計(可選) [circularCalculation]、圓環環環繞值( 可選) [circularWrapValue]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/raster-analysis/zonal-statistics.html#parameters

## 030148：所選測量單位 <value> 與阻抗 <value> 不兼容。 它們既不是時間值也不是距離值。 · 描述

**id**　0e6e04a09c573a8810-1

**english**　030148: The chosen measurement units, <value>, and impedance, <value>, are incompatible. They are not mutually time or distance values.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 30101-30200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/030001-040000/tool-errors-and-warnings-30126-30150-030148.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/030001-040000/tool-errors-and-warnings-30126-30150-030148.html

**text**　如果測量單位是基于時間的單位，則指定的阻抗屬性必須具有基于時間的單位。 如果測量單位是基于距離的單位，則指定的阻抗屬性必須具有基于距離的單位。

**source_language**　zh-cn

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 045075: 至少有兩個模型是相同的。 · 描述

**id**　0e84be0534896e2548-1

**english**　045075: At least two models are identical.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 45001-45100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45051-45075-045075.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45051-45075-045075.html

**text**　在 輸入地統計圖層 參數中提供的地統計模型中，至少有兩個地統計模型是相同的。 無論提供的條件為何，這些模型都會接收到相同的等級。
如果兩個模型共享相同的資料源（對于大多數插值方法，共享輸入點圖徵類和欄位）并且使用具有相同參數的相同插值方法，那么這兩個模型就是相同的。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45051-45075-045075.html#description

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 用法

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-2

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**text**　當輸入圖徵參數值為點時，事件類型值必須設置為點。 當輸入圖徵參數值為線或面時，事件類型值必須設置為線。
在輸入圖徵與目標路徑精確重疊時可取得最佳結果。 注意： 如果使用較大的搜索半徑或聚類容差克服輸入圖徵與目標路徑之間的偏差，則可能產生意想不到的結果。
要減少此工具處理的輸入圖徵的數量，可以輸入能夠執行選擇操作的圖層。
可使用創建路徑事件圖層工具將輸出表顯示在地圖中。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#usage

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-7

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具

**parameter**　radius_or_tolerance

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 搜索半徑
- 如果輸入圖徵參數值是點，則搜索半徑是數值，定義可在每個點周圍的多大范圍內執行搜索以找到目標路徑。 如果輸入圖徵參數值是線，則搜索容差是聚類容差，即表示輸入線與目標路徑之間的最大容許距離的數值。 如果輸入圖徵參數值是面，則忽略此參數且不使用搜索半徑。
- Linear Unit

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 參數

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-9

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**parameter**　out_event_properties

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 輸出事件表屬性
- 將寫入輸出事件表的路徑位置欄位和事件類型。 路徑標識符欄位—該欄位將包含指示每個事件所在路徑的值。 此欄位可以是數值、文本或 GUID 欄位。 事件類型—輸出事件表中將包含的事件類型（點或線）。 點—點事件出現在沿路徑的確切點位置處。 僅可指定只有單一測量欄位。 線—線事件定義路徑的一部分。 “測量始于”和“測量止于”都是必須指定的欄位。 測量始于欄位—將包含測量值的欄位。 當事件類型是點或線時，此欄位為必填欄位。 在事件類型值為點時，此參數的標注將變為測量欄位。 測量止于欄位—將包含測量值的欄位。 當事件類型是線時，此欄位為必填欄位。
- Route Measure Event Properties

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 參數

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-10

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**parameter**　route_locations

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 僅保留最近的路徑位置 （可選）
- 指定是否將最近的路線位置或搜索半徑內的每個路線位置寫入輸出事件表參數值。 在沿路線定位點時，任何給定點的搜索半徑內可能有不止一條路線。 沿路徑定位線或面時將忽略此參數。 選中—只將最近的路徑位置寫入輸出事件表參數值。 這是默認設置。 未選中—將每個路徑位置（搜索半徑范圍內）都寫入輸出事件表參數值。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

- 在輸出表中包括距離欄位 （可選）
- 指定是否將 DISTANCE 欄位添加到輸出事件表參數值。 此欄位中的值以指定的搜索半徑為單位。 沿路徑定位線或面時將忽略此參數。 選中—將包含點到路徑距離的欄位添加到輸出事件表參數值。 這是默認設置。 未選中—不會將包含點到路徑距離的欄位添加到輸出事件表參數值。
- Boolean

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 參數

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-14

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**parameter**　m_direction_offsetting

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 使用 M 方向偏移 （可選）
- 指定計算的偏移距離是基于 m 方向還是數字化方向。 如果選中輸出表包括距離欄位參數，則距離將包含在輸出事件表參數值中。 選中—將根據路徑的 M 方向計算輸出事件表參數值中的距離值。 位于路徑 M 方向左側的輸入圖徵將被賦予正 (+) 偏移，位于 M 方向右側的圖徵將被賦予負 (-) 偏移。 這是默認設置。 未選中—將根據路徑的數字化方向計算輸出事件表參數值中的距離值。 位于路徑數字化方向左側的輸入圖徵將被賦予負 (-) 偏移，位于路徑數字化方向右側的圖徵將被賦予正 (+) 偏移。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- radius_or_tolerance
- 如果 in_features 參數值是點，則搜索半徑是數值，定義可在每個點周圍的多大范圍內執行搜索以找到目標路徑。 如果 in_features 參數值是線，則搜索容差是聚類容差，即表示輸入線與目標路徑之間的最大容許距離的數值。 如果 in_features 參數值是面，則忽略此參數且不使用搜索半徑。
- Linear Unit

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 參數

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-21

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**parameter**　out_event_properties

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- out_event_properties
- 將寫入輸出事件表的路徑位置欄位和事件類型。 Route identifier field—該欄位將包含指示每個事件所在路徑的值。 此欄位可以是數值、文本或 GUID 欄位。 Event type—輸出事件表中將包含的事件類型（POINT 或 LINE）。 POINT—點事件出現在沿路徑的確切位置處。 僅可指定只有單一測量欄位。 LINE—線事件定義路徑的一部分。 “測量始于”和“測量止于”都是必須指定的欄位。 From-measure field—將包含測量值的欄位。 當事件類型是 POINT 或 LINE 時，此欄位為必填欄位。 To-measure field—將包含測量值的欄位。 當事件類型是 LINE 時，此欄位為必填欄位。
- Route Measure Event Properties

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 參數

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-22

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**parameter**　route_locations

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- route_locations （可選）
- 指定是否將最近的路線位置或搜索半徑內的每個路線位置寫入 out_table 參數值。 在沿路線定位點時，任何給定點的搜索半徑內可能有不止一條路線。 沿路徑定位線或面時將忽略此參數。 FIRST—只將最近的路徑位置寫入 out_table 參數值。 這是默認設置。 ALL—將每個路徑位置（搜索半徑范圍內）都寫入 out_table 參數值。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**text**　# Name: LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes_Example2.py
# Description: Locate shapefile lines along shapefile routes.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Data"

# Set local variables
feats = "rail_segments.shp"
rts = "rail_routes.shp"
rid = "rkey"
tol = "0.5 Feet"
tbl = "locate_lines"
props = "rkey LINE fmp tmp"

# Run LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes
arcpy.lr.LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes(feats, rts, rid, tol, tbl, props)

import arcpy

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Data/Pitt.gdb"

# Set local variables
feats = "rail/crossings"  # Crossings is in the rail feature dataset
rts = "rail/routes"  # Routes is in the rail feature dataset
rid = "rkey"
rad = "10 Feet"
tbl = "locate_points"
props = "rkey POINT mp"

# Run LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes
arcpy.lr.LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes(feats, rts, rid, rad, tbl, props)

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-34

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

- 線性參考工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**text**　# Name: LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes_Example4.py
# Description: Locate enterprise geodatabase polygons along enterprise geodatabase routes.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/MyProject/myConn.sde"

# Set local variables
feats = arcpy.ValidateTableName("counties", wkspc)  # Stand-alone feature class
rts = arcpy.ValidateTableName("rail_routes", wkspc)  # Stand-alone feature class
rid = "rkey"
tbl = "locate_polys"
props = "rkey LINE fmp tmp"

# Run LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes
arcpy.lr.LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes(feats, rts, rid, "#", tbl, props, "#", "#",
                                   "NO_ZERO", "M_DIRECTON")

## 沿路徑定位圖徵 (線性參考工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 線性參考工具箱

**english**　Locate Features Along Routes

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html

**id**　0e87638df2de2c3c0c-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Locate Features Along Routes，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、輸入路徑圖徵 [in_routes]、路徑標識符欄位 [route_id_field]、搜索半徑 [radius_or_tolerance]、輸出事件表 [out_table]、輸出事件表屬性 [out_event_properties]、僅保留最近的路徑位置 （可選） [route_locations]、在輸出表中包括距離欄位 （可選） [distance_field]、保留零長度線事件 （可選） [zero_length_events]、包括輸入的所有欄位 （可選） [in_fields]、使用 M 方向偏移 （可選） [m_direction_offsetting]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/linear-referencing/locate-features-along-routes.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入圖徵", "label_en": "Input Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input point, line, or polygon features."}, {"parameter": "in_routes", "label_zh": "輸入路徑圖徵", "label_en": "Input Route Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The routes with which the Input Features parameter value will intersect."}, {"parameter": "route_id_field", "label_zh": "路徑標識符欄位", "label_en": "Route Identifier Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field containing values that uniquely identify each route. The field can be a numeric, text, or GUID field."}, {"parameter": "radius_or_tolerance", "label_zh": "搜索半徑", "label_en": "Search Radius", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "If the Input Features parameter value is points, the search radius will be a numeric value defining how far around each point a search will be done to find a target route. If the Input Features parameter value is lines, the search tolerance will be a cluster tolerance, which is a numeric value representing the maximum tolerated distance between the input lines and the target routes. If the Input Features parameter value is polygons, this parameter is ignored and no search radius will be used."}, {"parameter": "out_table", "label_zh": "輸出事件表", "label_en": "Output Event Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The table that will be created. The output table can be a dBase (.dbf file) or a geodatabase table."}, {"parameter": "out_event_properties", "label_zh": "輸出事件表屬性", "label_en": "Output Event Table Properties", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Route Measure Event Properties", "description_en": "The route location fields and the type of events that will be written to the output event table. Route Identifier Field—The field that will contain values that indicate the route on which each event is located. The field can be a numeric, text, or GUID field. Event Type—The type of events the output event table will contain (Point or Line). Point—Point events occur at a precise location along a route. Only a single measure field must be specified. Line—Line events define a portion of a route. Both from- and to-measure fields must be specified. From-Measure Field—A field that will contain measure values. This field is required when the event type is Point or Line. When the Event Type value is Point, the label for this parameter becomes Measure Field. To-Measure Field—A field that will contain measure values. This field is required when the event type is Line."}, {"parameter": "route_locations", "label_zh": "僅保留最近的路徑位置 （可選）", "label_en": "Keep only the closest route location (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the closest route location or every route location within the search radius will be written to the Output Event Table parameter value. When locating points along routes, more than one route may be within the search radius of any given point. This parameter is ignored when locating lines or polygons along routes. Checked—Only the closest route location will be written to the Output Event Table parameter value. This is the default. Unchecked—Every route location within the search radius will be written to the Output Event Table parameter value."}, {"parameter": "distance_field", "label_zh": "在輸出表中包括距離欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Include distance field on output table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether a DISTANCE field will be added to the Output Event Table parameter value. The values in this field are in the units of the specified search radius. This parameter is ignored when locating lines or polygons along routes. Checked—A field containing the point-to-route distance will be added to the Output Event Table parameter value. This is the default. Unchecked—A field containing the point-to-route distance will not be added to the Output Event Table parameter value."}, {"parameter": "zero_length_events", "label_zh": "保留零長度線事件 （可選）", "label_en": "Keep zero length line events (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether zero-length line events will be written to the output. When locating polygons along routes, events may be created where the from-measure is equal to the to-measure. This parameter is ignored when locating points or lines along routes. Checked—Zero-length line events will be written to the Output Event Table parameter value. This is the default. Unchecked—Zero-length line events will not be written to the Output Event Table parameter value."}, {"parameter": "in_fields", "label_zh": "包括輸入的所有欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Include all fields from input (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the Output Event Table parameter value will contain route location fields and all the attributes from the Input Features parameter value. Checked—The Output Event Table parameter value will contain route location fields and all the attributes from the Input Features parameter value. This is the default. Unchecked—The Output Event Table parameter value will only contain route location fields and the ObjectID field from the Input Features parameter value."}, {"parameter": "m_direction_offsetting", "label_zh": "使用 M 方向偏移 （可選）", "label_en": "Use M direction offsetting (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the offset distance calculated will be based on the m-direction or the digitized direction. Distances are included in the Output Event Table parameter value if the Include distance field on output table parameter is checked. Checked—The distance values in the Output Event Table parameter value will be calculated based on the m-direction of the route. Input features to the left of the m-direction of the route will be assigned a positive (+) offset, and features to the right of the m-direction will be assigned a negative (-) offset. This is the default. Unchecked—The distance values in the Output Event Table parameter value will be calculated based on the digitized direction of the route. Input features to the left of the digitized direction of the route will be assigned a negative (-) offset, and features to the right of the digitized direction will be assigned a positive (+) offset."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.lr.LocateFeaturesAlongRoutes(in_features, in_routes, route_id_field, radius_or_tolerance, out_table, out_event_properties, {route_locations}, {distance_field}, {zero_length_events}, {in_fields}, {m_direction_offsetting})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、in_routes、route_id_field、radius_or_tolerance、out_table、out_event_properties、route_locations、distance_field、zero_length_events、in_fields、m_direction_offsetting。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 輻射粗體如何起作用 · 輻射粗體如何起作用

**id**　0e8940b13ac16ab285-1

**english**　How Radial Roughness works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 表面工具板
- 表面工具概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html

**text**　使用空間分析員的許可證。 輻射粗糙工具使用半徑粗糙指數法(TRevisani等人,2023年)計算輸入光柵每個單元格的短程全向粗糙值。 輸出粗糙值是測量當前單元格與其相鄰單元格之間高度差異的一個尺度, 具有以下特征: 輸出粗糙值獨立于本地斜坡。 在計算過程中, 使用雙線內插法按照正向方向調整單元格中心之間的距離。 輸出光柵的最低值為0, 代表一個平滑的區域。 一個更崎嶇的區域將具有更高的價值。 以下一些應用實例如下:使用產出粗糙度光柵:在數字高地模型上,利用粗粗度值查明粗和平滑地形

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Available with the Spatial Analyst license.\nThe Radial Roughness tool calculates a short-range omnidirectional roughness value for each cell of an input surface raster, using the radial roughness index approach (Trevisani, et al, 2023). The output roughness value is a measure of the height differences between the current cell and its neighboring cells, with the following characteristics:\nThe output roughness value is independent of the local slope.\nDuring the calculation, the distance between cell centers is adjusted along the ordinal directions using bilinear interpolation.\nThe lowest possible value on the output raster is 0, which represents a smooth area. A more rugged area will have a higher value.\nThe following are some application examples using the output roughness raster:\nIdentifying rough and smooth terrains on a digital elevation model (DEM) using the roughness values\nUsing it as a layer in land use suitability modeling", "section": "How Radial Roughness works"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html

**text**　對于任何給定輸出單元格,工具根據兩個不同區域大小的12個方向差異計算最后粗略值。要做到這一點,工具要經歷以下步驟:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For any given output cell, the tool calculates a final roughness value from 12 directional differences from two different neighborhood sizes. To do this, the tool goes through the following steps:", "section": "Radial roughness algorithm"}

## 輻射粗體如何起作用 · 輻射粗野算法

**id**　0e8940b13ac16ab285-4

**english**　How Radial Roughness works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 表面工具板
- 表面工具概念

**kind**　有序步驟

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html#XVR

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html

**steps**　
1. 對于5乘5的囚室鄰居, 計算二階差值在8個方向。
2. 對于3乘3的牢房鄰居,從四個方向計算二階差值。
3. 計算最后粗略值時,平均前兩個步驟產生的12個數值。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"steps": ["For a 5-by-5 cell neighborhood, calculate the second-order difference values in eight directions.", "For a 3-by-3 cell neighborhood, calculate the second-order difference values in four directions.", "Calculate the final roughness value by averaging the 12 values resulting from the previous two steps."], "section": "Radial roughness algorithm"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html#XVR

**id**　0e8940b13ac16ab285-6

**english**　How Radial Roughness works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html#HQF

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html

**text**　兩個相鄰單元格之間的值差異是一階差。第二階差是兩對相鄰單元格的單元格值差異的差異。在計算單元格值差異時,該工具使用二階差法。它允許在最后輸出粗略值中去除本地斜度的影響。以下各節介紹了工具用來計算5乘5鄰內八個方向差異的公式。公式中使用的具體單元格按其列和行分列如下:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The difference in values between two adjacent cells is a first-order difference. A second-order difference is the difference in the differences in the cell values between two adjacent pairs of cells. This tool uses a second-order difference approach when computing the cell value differences. It allows the effect of the local slope to be removed in the final output roughness values. The following sections present the formulas used by the tool to compute the differences along the eight directions for a 5-by-5 neighborhood.\nThe specific cells used in the formulas are identified by their column and row as follows:", "section": "Compute second-order differences in the 5-by-5 neighborhood"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html#HQF

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html#FKY

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html

**text**　四個主方向是東、南、西和北。以鄰里東方向的單元格為例,該方向第二階差的絕對值計算如下:\(D_1)= ⁇ ( ⁇ D3}- ⁇ E3})-( ⁇ C3}- ⁇ D3})-( ⁇ 2****D3}- ⁇ C3}-( ⁇ C3})Z是鄰里某個單元格的單元格值。同樣,在南方方向,公式如下:\(D_2)= ⁇ 2 * ⁇ C4}-( ⁇ C3)。-==YTET -伊甸園字幕組=- 翻譯:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The four cardinal directions are East, South, West, and North. Using the cells in the East direction of the neighborhood as an example, the absolute value of the second-order difference for that direction is calculated as follows:\n\\(D_1 = | (Z_{D3} - Z_{E3}) - (Z_{C3} - Z_{D3}) | = | 2 * Z_{D3} - Z_{C3} - Z_{E3} |\\)\nWhere Z is the cell value for a given cell in the neighborhood.\nSimilarly, for the South direction, the formula is as follows:\n\\(D_2 = | 2 * Z_{C4} - Z_{C3} - Z_{C5} |\\)\nFor the West direction:\n\\(D_3 = | 2 * Z_{B3} - Z_{C3} - Z_{A3} |\\)\nFor the North direction:\n\\(D_4 = | 2 * Z_{C2} - Z_{C3} - Z_{C1} |\\)", "section": "Second-order differences in the cardinal directions"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-radial-roughness-works.html#FKY

**id**　0e8940b13ac16ab285-8

**text**　4個正弦方向是東北、東南、西南和西北。 由于細胞中心之間的距離大于沿正弦方向的一個單元格, 使用雙線內插法將細胞值沿著這些方向與1個單元格的距離取得。 以東北方向為例, 考慮兩線內插權重, 以下是計算該方向第二階差的公式(Trevisani等人, 2023年):\ (D_ 5 =  ⁇  - 0.5 * *  ⁇ C3} - 0.5 * {E1} - 0.271 *  ⁇ C2} - 0.271 *  ⁇ D1} - 0.271* 0.2071* 。”D3} - 0.271 * * {E2} + 1.8284} * Z 代表附近某個單元格的單元格值。同樣,東南方向的公式如下:\\(D_6= ⁇ -0.5* ⁇ C3} - 0.5 * * * E5} - 0.271 * * * D3} - 0.271 * * * E4} - 0.2071 * * * * * C4} - 0.271 * * * * d5} - 0.271 * * * * * D5}+ 1.8284 * * * D4} }西南方向:

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The four ordinal directions are Northeast, Southeast, Southwest, and Northwest. Because the distance between cell centers is greater than one cell along the ordinal directions, bilinear interpolation is used to get the cell values along these directions with the distance of 1 cell.\nUsing the Northeast direction as an example, with the bilinear interpolation weights considered, the following is the formula for calculating the second-order difference in that direction (Trevisani, et al, 2023):\n\\(D_5 = | -0.5 * Z_{C3} - 0.5 * Z_{E1} - 0.2071 * Z_{C2} - 0.2071 * Z_{D1} - 0.2071 * Z_{D3} - 0.2071 * Z_{E2} + 1.8284 * Z_{D2} |\\)\nWhere Z represents the cell value for a given cell in the neighborhood.\nSimilarly, for the Southeast direction, the formula is as follows:\n\\(D_6 = | -0.5 * Z_{C3} - 0.5 * Z_{E5} - 0.2071 * Z_{D3} - 0.2071 * Z_{E4} - 0.2071 * Z_{C4} - 0.2071 * Z_{D5} + 1.8284 * Z_{D4} |\\)\nFor the Southwest direction:", "section": "Second-order differences in the ordinal directions"}

**content_version**　3.8

**text**　在3x3鄰里,計算差異的四個方向是東西、南北、東北-西南和西北-東南。以下是這些方向的公式。東-西方向:\(D_9)= ⁇ 2 * {C3} - ⁇ B3} - ⁇ D3} - ⁇ D3}}})北-南方向:\(D) ⁇ 10}= ⁇ 2* *C3} - ⁇ C2} - ⁇ C4}}__}(C4}}}}}}}東北-西方向:\(D)11}= ⁇ -0.5* B4}―0.5* *D2} - 0.271* * B3} - 0.271 * * C2} - 0.271 * * * C4} - 0.271 * * * * * C4} - 0.271 * * * D3} + 1.8284 * * * C3} * 西北-東南部方向:\ (D) * = =  ⁇  0.5 * * * D4} - 0.5 * * * B4} - 0.271 * * * C4} - 0.271 * * * * B3} - - 0.771 * * * * D3} - 0.2071 * * * * * C2} + 1.8284 * * * * * C3} )

The following are the formulas for those.\nFor the East-West direction:\n\\(D_9 = | 2 * Z_{C3} - Z_{B3} – Z_{D3} |\\)\nFor the North-South direction:\n\\(D_{10} = | 2 * Z_{C3} - Z_{C2} – Z_{C4} |\\)\nFor the Northeast-Southwest direction:\n\\(D_{11} = | -0.5 * Z_{B4} - 0.5 * Z_{D2} - 0.2071 * Z_{B3} - 0.2071 * Z_{C2} - 0.2071 * Z_{C4} - 0.2071 * Z_{D3} + 1.8284 * Z_{C3} |\\)\nFor the Northwest-Southeast direction:\n\\(D_{12} = | -0.5 * Z_{D4} - 0.5 * Z_{B2} - 0.2071 * Z_{C4} - 0.2071 * Z_{B3} - 0.2071 * Z_{D3} - 0.2071 * Z_{C2} + 1.8284 * Z_{C3} |\\)", "section": "Compute second-order differences in the 3-by-3 neighborhood"}
